For protein kinases, the metal-binding sites typically involve:
1. The catalytic aspartate (D) of the HRD motif - binds metal 1 (Mg²⁺)
2. The D of the DFG motif - binds metal 2 (Mg²⁺ or Mn²⁺)
3. The N of the catalytic loop (after HRD) - coordinates the substrate phosphate

In many kinases, both Mg²⁺ and Mn²⁺ can bind at the same sites (they&#x27;re both divalent cations that can be used by kinases). The DFG aspartate is known to coordinate both Mg²⁺ and Mn²⁺ in many kinases.
